{

Fold weak offsuit junk, especially out of early position. Call raises only with strong hands or good odds.\nPostflop: bet/raise when you hold top pair or better or a strong draw; check-call medium hands; fold marginal hands facing multiple bets. Don't bluff into several opponents; bluff semi-selectively with draws in position.\nPosition matters: widen range on the button and cutoff, tighten in early seats.\nTrack opponents: note who folds to aggression, who calls too much, and adjust (value-bet thin against calling stations, bluff more against tight folders).\nNever chase weak draws across multiple bets."
}
```
